  We introduce the core abstractions and architectural elements required to realize APM systems and elaborate on four key capabilities that such APM agents must support: framed autonomy, explainability, conversational actionability, and self-modification. These capabilities jointly ensure that agents' goals are aligned with organizational goals and that agents behave in a framed yet proactive manner in pursuing those goals. We discuss the extent to which the capabilities can be realized and identify research challenges whose resolution requires further advances in BPM, AI, and multi-agent systems. The manifesto thus serves as a roadmap for bridging these communities and for guiding the development of APM systems in practice.

</details>


### [189] [Teleological Inference in Structural Causal Models via Intentional Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18968)
*Dario Compagno,Fabio Massimo Zennaro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新方法，将结构因果模型（SCMs）扩展到能够处理目的论问题，通过引入"意图干预"操作符和结构最终模型（SFMs）来检测智能体及其意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统结构因果模型主要用于回答因果问题，但无法处理涉及有意识、目标导向智能体的目的论问题。现有方法在建模这类智能体时存在局限性，需要新的框架来分析智能体的意图和干预行为。

Method: 引入"意图干预"这一时间无关的操作符，该操作符诱导出一个称为"结构最终模型"的双胞胎SCM。SFMs将观测值视为意图干预的结果，并将其与这些干预的反事实条件（如果智能体不干预会发生什么）联系起来。

Result: 提出的结构最终模型能够从经验上检测系统中是否存在智能体，并发现这些智能体的意图。该方法为分析目标导向智能体的行为提供了新的理论框架。

Conclusion: 结构因果模型不仅可以用于因果分析，还可以扩展到目的论问题的研究。通过引入意图干预和结构最终模型，能够形式化地分析智能体的意图和行为，为理解目标导向系统的干预行为提供了新工具。

Abstract: Structural causal models (SCMs) were conceived to formulate and answer causal questions. This paper shows that SCMs can also be used to formulate and answer teleological questions, concerning the intentions of a state-aware, goal-directed agent intervening in a causal system. We review limitations of previous approaches to modeling such agents, and then introduce intentional interventions, a new time-agnostic operator that induces a twin SCM we call a structural final model (SFM). SFMs treat observed values as the outcome of intentional interventions and relate them to the counterfactual conditions of those interventions (what would have happened had the agent not intervened). We show how SFMs can be used to empirically detect agents and to discover their intentions.

</details>


### [190] [Evaluating 5W3H Structured Prompting for Intent Alignment in Human-AI Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18976)
*Peng Gang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PPS（Prompt Protocol Specification）框架使用5W3H结构化意图表示，在人类-AI交互中能显著提升意图对齐，特别是在高模糊性任务中效果明显，但低模糊性任务中可能适得其反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言提示常存在意图传输损失问题——用户实际需求与传达给AI系统之间的差距。需要结构化框架来改善人类-AI交互中的意图对齐。

Method: 采用三条件对照研究：60个任务（商业、技术、旅行领域），三个大语言模型（DeepSeek-V3、Qwen-Max、Kimi），三种提示条件（简单提示、原始PPS JSON、自然语言渲染的PPS）。收集540个AI输出，由LLM评估器评估，引入goal_alignment维度，并进行初步回顾性调查（N=20）。

Result: 渲染后的PPS在意图对齐指标上优于简单提示和原始JSON；PPS效果具有任务依赖性：高模糊性商业分析任务中提升显著，低模糊性旅行规划中效果反转；发现标准LLM评估存在测量不对称性；初步调查显示后续提示轮数减少66.1%（从3.33轮降至1.13轮）。

Conclusion: 结构化意图表示能改善人类-AI交互中的对齐和可用性，特别是在用户意图固有模糊的任务中效果显著，但需注意任务依赖性。

Abstract: Natural language prompts often suffer from intent transmission loss: the gap between what users actually need and what they communicate to AI systems. We evaluate PPS (Prompt Protocol Specification), a 5W3H-based framework for structured intent representation in human-AI interaction. In a controlled three-condition study across 60 tasks in three domains (business, technical, and travel), three large language models (DeepSeek-V3, Qwen-Max, and Kimi), and three prompt conditions - (A) simple prompts, (B) raw PPS JSON, and (C) natural-language-rendered PPS - we collect 540 AI-generated outputs evaluated by an LLM judge. We introduce goal_alignment, a user-intent-centered evaluation dimension, and find that rendered PPS outperforms both simple prompts and raw JSON on this metric. PPS gains are task-dependent: gains are large in high-ambiguity business analysis tasks but reverse in low-ambiguity travel planning. We also identify a measurement asymmetry in standard LLM evaluation, where unconstrained prompts can inflate constraint adherence scores and mask the practical value of structured prompting. A preliminary retrospective survey (N = 20) further suggests a 66.1% reduction in follow-up prompts required, from 3.33 to 1.13 rounds. These findings suggest that structured intent representations can improve alignment and usability in human-AI interaction, especially in tasks where user intent is inherently ambiguous.

</details>


### [191] [Unmasking Algorithmic Bias in Predictive Policing: A GAN-Based Simulation Framework with Multi-City Temporal Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18987)
*Pronob Kumar Barman,Pronoy Kumar Barman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 开发可重复模拟框架，量化预测警务系统中种族偏见的传播与放大效应


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测警务系统在美国城市广泛部署，但其编码和放大种族差异的倾向在量化层面仍未被充分理解，需要系统性的测量框架

Method: 结合生成对抗网络(GAN)和Noisy OR巡逻检测模型的可重复模拟框架，使用巴尔的摩和芝加哥的犯罪记录与人口普查数据，计算四种月度偏见指标

Result: 巴尔的摩检测模式存在极端且年度变化的偏见，芝加哥存在对黑人居民的适度欠检测，所有条件下基尼系数持续偏高；CTGAN去偏方法只能部分重新分配检测率

Conclusion: 预测警务系统会显著放大种族差异，单纯的技术去偏方法无法消除结构性不平等，需要配套政策干预；警官部署水平对结果影响最大

Abstract: Predictive policing systems that direct patrol resources based on algorithmically generated crime forecasts have been widely deployed across US cities, yet their tendency to encode and amplify racial disparities remains poorly understood in quantitative terms. We present a reproducible simulation framework that couples a Generative Adversarial Network GAN with a Noisy OR patrol detection model to measure how racial bias propagates through the full enforcement pipeline from crime occurrence to police contact. Using 145000 plus Part 1 crime records from Baltimore 2017 to 2019 and 233000 plus records from Chicago 2022, augmented with US Census ACS demographic data, we compute four monthly bias metrics across 264 city year mode observations: the Disparate Impact Ratio DIR, Demographic Parity Gap, Gini Coefficient, and a composite Bias Amplification Score.
  Our experiments reveal extreme and year variant bias in Baltimores detected mode, with mean annual DIR up to 15714 in 2019, moderate under detection of Black residents in Chicago DIR equals 0.22, and persistent Gini coefficients of 0.43 to 0.62 across all conditions. We further demonstrate that a Conditional Tabular GAN CTGAN debiasing approach partially redistributes detection rates but cannot eliminate structural disparity without accompanying policy intervention. Socioeconomic regression analysis confirms strong correlations between neighborhood racial composition and detection likelihood Pearson r equals 0.83 for percent White and r equals negative 0.81 for percent Black. A sensitivity analysis over patrol radius, officer count, and citizen reporting probability reveals that outcomes are most sensitive to officer deployment levels. The code and data are publicly available at this repository.

</details>


### [192] [Evaluating Game Difficulty in Tetris Block Puzzle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18994)
*Chun-Jui Wang,Jian-Ting Guo,Hung Guei,Chung-Chin Shih,Ti-Rong Wu,I-Chen Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 使用Stochastic Gumbel AlphaZero评估Tetris Block Puzzle变体的难度，发现增加持有块和预览块降低难度，添加更多方块变体增加难度，T-五连块最难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tetris Block Puzzle及其变体拥有数千万下载量，但缺乏对不同规则集难度的系统评估。需要一种原则性的方法来评估哪些规则集更难，为游戏设计提供参考。

Method: 使用Stochastic Gumbel AlphaZero（SGAZ）作为预算感知的随机环境规划代理，评估不同规则变化：持有块数量h、预览块数量p、以及不同的Tetris方块变体。通过训练奖励和收敛迭代等指标来衡量难度。

Result: 增加持有块h和预览块p会降低难度（获得更高奖励和更快收敛），而添加更多Tetris方块变体会增加难度，其中T-五连块导致最大的训练速度减慢。SGAZ在小模拟预算下表现出色，能够高效、可重复地比较不同规则集。

Conclusion: SGAZ为随机拼图游戏提供了一种有效的难度评估方法，能够高效比较不同规则集，为未来游戏设计提供参考依据。该方法特别适用于预算受限的环境，实现了可重复的规则集比较。

Abstract: Tetris Block Puzzle is a single player stochastic puzzle in which a player places blocks on an 8 x 8 grid to complete lines; its popular variants have amassed tens of millions of downloads. Despite this reach, there is little principled assessment of which rule sets are more difficult. Inspired by prior work that uses AlphaZero as a strong evaluator for chess variants, we study difficulty in this domain using Stochastic Gumbel AlphaZero (SGAZ), a budget-aware planning agent for stochastic environments. We evaluate rule changes including holding block h, preview holding block p, and additional Tetris block variants using metrics such as training reward and convergence iterations. Empirically, increasing h and p reduces difficulty (higher reward and faster convergence), while adding more Tetris block variants increases difficulty, with the T-pentomino producing the largest slowdown. Through analysis, SGAZ delivers strong play under small simulation budgets, enabling efficient, reproducible comparisons across rule sets and providing a reference for future design in stochastic puzzle games.

</details>


### [193] [Regret Bounds for Competitive Resource Allocation with Endogenous Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18999)
*Rui Chai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究具有内生成本的在线资源分配问题，通过三种分配策略（均匀、门控、竞争）在对抗序列下建立严格性能分离，并证明交互矩阵拓扑结构影响计算-遗憾权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究模块化系统中在线资源分配问题，其中成本是内生的——依赖于通过交互矩阵编码的模块间合作与竞争关系。这与标准在线优化不同，需要处理成本内生性这一根本挑战。

Method: 分析三种分配范式：I) 均匀分配（忽略成本）；II) 门控分配（估计成本）；III) 竞争分配（通过带交互反馈的乘性权重更新）。在对抗序列下分析遗憾界，并研究交互矩阵W的拓扑结构对计算-遗憾权衡的影响。

Result: 在有限变化的对抗序列下建立严格分离：均匀分配有Ω(T)遗憾，门控分配达到O(T^{2/3})，竞争分配达到O(√(T log N))。交互拓扑结构影响权衡：完全交互（O(N²)边）有最紧界但计算成本最高；稀疏拓扑（O(N)边）最多增加O(√(log N))遗憾但计算成本降至O(N)。环状拓扑（如五行拓扑）最小化计算×遗憾乘积。

Conclusion: 首次为模块化架构中的去中心化竞争分配提供形式化的遗憾理论依据，确立成本内生性是与部分可观测性不同的根本挑战。交互拓扑结构在计算效率和遗憾性能间提供可调权衡。

Abstract: We study online resource allocation among N interacting modules over T rounds. Unlike standard online optimization, costs are endogenous: they depend on the full allocation vector through an interaction matrix W encoding pairwise cooperation and competition.
  We analyze three paradigms: (I) uniform allocation (cost-ignorant), (II) gated allocation (cost-estimating), and (III) competitive allocation via multiplicative weights update with interaction feedback (cost-revealing). Our main results establish a strict separation under adversarial sequences with bounded variation: uniform incurs Omega(T) regret, gated achieves O(T^{2/3}), and competitive achieves O(sqrt(T log N)). The performance gap stems from competitive allocation's ability to exploit endogenous cost information revealed through interactions.
  We further show that W's topology governs a computation-regret tradeoff. Full interaction (|E|=O(N^2)) yields the tightest bound but highest per-step cost, while sparse topologies (|E|=O(N)) increase regret by at most O(sqrt(log N)) while reducing per-step cost from O(N^2) to O(N). Ring-structured topologies with both cooperative and competitive links - of which the five-element Wuxing topology is canonical - minimize the computation x regret product.
  These results provide the first formal regret-theoretic justification for decentralized competitive allocation in modular architectures and establish cost endogeneity as a fundamental challenge distinct from partial observability.
  Keywords: online learning, regret bounds, resource allocation, endogenous costs, interaction topology, multiplicative weights, modular systems, Wuxing topology

</details>


### [194] [Behavioral Fingerprints for LLM Endpoint Stability and Identity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19022)
*Jonah Leshin,Manish Shah,Ian Timmis,Daniel Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Stability Monitor系统，通过固定提示集采样输出并比较分布变化来监控AI模型端点的行为一致性，检测模型更新、量化、推理栈等变化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可靠性指标（正常运行时间、延迟、吞吐量）无法捕捉模型行为变化，模型端点可能在保持"健康"状态时，由于权重、分词器、量化、推理引擎等更新而改变其有效模型身份，影响AI原生应用的一致性

Method: 使用黑盒稳定性监控系统，定期通过固定提示集对端点进行指纹采集，采样输出并比较时间上的输出分布变化。使用跨提示的求和能量距离统计量比较指纹，通过排列检验p值作为分布偏移证据，顺序聚合以检测变化事件和定义稳定期

Result: 在受控验证中，Stability Monitor成功检测到模型家族、版本、推理栈、量化和行为参数的变化。在多个提供商托管同一模型的真实监控中，观察到显著的提供商间和提供商内稳定性差异

Conclusion: Stability Monitor提供了一种有效的方法来监控AI模型端点的行为一致性，弥补了传统可靠性指标的不足，有助于确保AI原生应用的稳定性和可预测性

Abstract: The consistency of AI-native applications depends on the behavioral consistency of the model endpoints that power them. Traditional reliability metrics such as uptime, latency and throughput do not capture behavioral change, and an endpoint can remain "healthy" while its effective model identity changes due to updates to weights, tokenizers, quantization, inference engines, kernels, caching, routing, or hardware. We introduce Stability Monitor, a black-box stability monitoring system that periodically fingerprints an endpoint by sampling outputs from a fixed prompt set and comparing the resulting output distributions over time. Fingerprints are compared using a summed energy distance statistic across prompts, with permutation-test p-values as evidence of distribution shift aggregated sequentially to detect change events and define stability periods. In controlled validation, Stability Monitor detects changes to model family, version, inference stack, quantization, and behavioral parameters. In real-world monitoring of the same model hosted by multiple providers, we observe substantial provider-to-provider and within-provider stability differences.

</details>


### [195] [Man and machine: artificial intelligence and judicial decision making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19042)
*Arthur Dyevre,Ahmad Shahvaroughi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文综述了AI在司法决策中的应用，特别是风险评估工具，分析了AI工具性能、法官偏见以及人机交互，发现AI对司法决策影响有限，呼吁更多跨学科研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI技术在司法决策（特别是审前、量刑和假释）中的应用引发了透明度、可靠性和问责制的担忧，同时也凸显了人类判断的局限性，需要理解法官如何与AI决策辅助工具互动。

Method: 使用刑事司法风险评估作为焦点案例，进行综合综述，连接AI工具性能与公平性、人类法官的优势与偏见、以及人机交互三个相互关联的方面，跨计算机科学、经济学、法学、犯罪学和心理学等多个领域。

Result: 现有经验证据表明AI决策辅助工具对审前和量刑决策的影响有限或不存在，但研究也揭示了文献中的重要空白，需要进一步评估AI风险评估工具的性能，理解法官如何在嘈杂决策环境中导航以及个体特征如何影响法官对AI建议的反应。

Conclusion: AI与人类的比较研究有潜力为算法工具和人类决策者提供新见解，倡导未来研究中更大的跨学科整合和交叉融合。

Abstract: The integration of artificial intelligence (AI) technologies into judicial decision-making - particularly in pretrial, sentencing, and parole contexts - has generated substantial concerns about transparency, reliability, and accountability. At the same time, these developments have brought the limitations of human judgment into sharper relief and underscored the importance of understanding how judges interact with AI-based decision aids. Using criminal justice risk assessment as a focal case, we conduct a synthetic review connecting three intertwined aspects of AI's role in judicial decision-making: the performance and fairness of AI tools, the strengths and biases of human judges, and the nature of AI+human interactions. Across the fields of computer science, economics, law, criminology and psychology, researchers have made significant progress in evaluating the predictive validity of automated risk assessment instruments, documenting biases in judicial decision-making, and, to a more limited extent, examining how judges use algorithmic recommendations. While the existing empirical evidence indicates that the impact of AI decision aid tools on pretrial and sentencing decisions is modest or inexistent, our review also reveals important gaps in the canvassed literatures. Further research is needed to evaluate the performance of AI risk assessment instruments, understand how judges navigate noisy decision making environments and how individual characteristics influence judges' responses to AI advice. We argue that AI vs Human comparisons have the potential to yield new insights into both algorithmic tools and human decision-makers and advocate greater interdisciplinary integration and cross-fertilization in future research.

</details>


### [196] [Serendipity by Design: Evaluating the Impact of Cross-domain Mappings on Human and LLM Creativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19087)
*Qiawen Ella Liu,Marina Dubova,Henry Conklin,Takumi Harada,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究比较人类和LLM在创造力干预上的差异，发现人类能从跨领域映射中获益，而LLM整体创造力更强但对跨领域映射无显著提升，但两者都随语义距离增加而效果增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM是否与人类具有相似的创造力机制，以及相同的创造力干预措施（跨领域映射）是否对两者都有效，从而理解人类与AI创造力的异同。

Method: 让人类参与者和LLM为日常产品生成创新特征，使用两种提示：跨领域映射（从随机分配的远程源领域获取灵感）和用户需求（针对未满足的用户需求提出创新）。

Result: 人类能可靠地从随机跨领域映射中获益，而LLM平均生成比人类更具原创性的想法，但对跨领域映射无统计显著效果。两者都随语义距离增加而效果增强。

Conclusion: 远程关联在创造性构思中起重要作用，但人类和LLM对相同创造力干预措施的反应存在系统性差异，揭示了两种系统在创造力机制上的不同。

Abstract: Are large language models (LLMs) creative in the same way humans are, and can the same interventions increase creativity in both? We evaluate a promising but largely untested intervention for creativity: forcing creators to draw an analogy from a random, remote source domain (''cross-domain mapping''). Human participants and LLMs generated novel features for ten daily products (e.g., backpack, TV) under two prompts: (i) cross-domain mapping, which required translating a property from a randomly assigned source (e.g., octopus, cactus, GPS), and (ii) user-need, which required proposing innovations targeting unmet user needs. We show that humans reliably benefit from randomly assigned cross-domain mappings, while LLMs, on average, generate more original ideas than humans and do not show a statistically significant effect of cross-domain mappings. However, in both systems, the impact of cross-domain mapping increases when the inspiration source becomes more semantically distant from the target. Our results highlight both the role of remote association in creative ideation and systematic differences in how humans and LLMs respond to the same intervention for creativity.

</details>


### [197] [LuMamba: Latent Unified Mamba for Electrode Topology-Invariant and Efficient EEG Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19100)
*Danaé Broustail,Anna Tegon,Thorir Mar Ingolfsson,Yawei Li,Luca Benini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LuMamba是一个用于EEG信号的自监督学习框架，结合拓扑不变编码和线性复杂度状态空间建模，在计算效率和性能上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型面临两大挑战：1)不同电极拓扑结构的差异导致模型难以统一处理；2)传统Transformer架构的二次序列复杂度限制了计算可扩展性。

Method: 提出LuMamba框架，结合LUNA的通道统一机制（学习查询交叉注意力）和FEMBA的双向Mamba块（线性复杂度状态空间建模），并首次系统研究LeJEPA架构在生物信号学习中的应用。

Result: 在21,000小时无标签EEG数据上预训练，仅4.6M参数，在TUAB任务上达到80.99%平衡准确率，阿尔茨海默症检测AUPR达0.97，计算效率提升377倍，序列长度扩展12倍。

Conclusion: LuMamba通过结合拓扑不变编码和线性复杂度建模，有效解决了EEG基础模型的计算可扩展性和电极拓扑差异问题，为生物信号处理提供了高效的自监督学习框架。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) enables non-invasive monitoring of brain activity across clinical and neurotechnology applications, yet building foundation models for EEG remains challenging due to \emph{differing electrode topologies} and \emph{computational scalability}, as Transformer architectures incur quadratic sequence complexity. As a joint solution, we propose \textbf{LuMamba} (\textbf{L}atent \textbf{U}nified \textbf{Mamba}), a self-supervised framework combining topology-invariant encodings with linear-complexity state-space modeling, using LUNA's learned-query cross-attention mechanism for channel unification~\cite{luna}, and FEMBA's bidirectional Mamba blocks for efficient temporal modeling~\cite{femba}. Within this architecture, we provide the first systematic investigation of the Latent-Euclidean Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (LeJEPA) for biosignal learning. Pre-trained on over 21,000 hours of unlabeled EEG from the TUEG corpus, LuMamba is evaluated on five downstream tasks spanning abnormality detection, artifact recognition, and mental condition classification across electrode configurations ranging from 16 to 26 channels. In the pre-training objective, masked reconstruction alone yields structured but less generalizable representations, while LeJEPA alone produces diffuse embeddings; combining both objectives achieves the most robust performance. With only 4.6M parameters, LuMamba attains 80.99\% balanced accuracy on TUAB and achieves state-of-art performance on Alzheimer's detection (0.97 AUPR), while requiring \textbf{377$\times$ fewer FLOPS} than state-of-art models at equivalent sequence lengths and scaling to \textbf{12$\times$ longer sequences} before reaching typical GPU memory limits. Code is available at https://github.com/pulp-bio/biofoundation

</details>


### [198] [How Uncertainty Estimation Scales with Sampling in Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19118)
*Maksym Del,Markus Kängsepp,Marharyta Domnich,Ardi Tampuu,Lisa Yankovskaya,Meelis Kull,Mark Fishel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究不确定性估计在链式推理语言模型中的表现，发现结合自我一致性和语言化置信度的混合方法在少量样本下就能显著提升性能，且数学领域表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不确定性估计对于部署推理语言模型至关重要，但在扩展的链式推理中仍然缺乏深入理解。需要研究并行采样作为完全黑盒方法，使用语言化置信度和自我一致性来评估不确定性。

Method: 采用并行采样作为完全黑盒方法，结合语言化置信度和自我一致性两种信号。在3个推理模型和17个任务（涵盖数学、STEM和人文学科）上进行实验，分析这些信号的扩展特性。

Result: 自我一致性和语言化置信度在推理模型中都能扩展，但自我一致性初始区分度较低，在中等采样下落后于语言化置信度。混合估计器仅用2个样本就能将AUROC平均提升高达+12分，即使扩展到更大样本预算也优于单一信号。数学领域的不确定性质量最高，表现出更强的互补性和更快的扩展性。

Conclusion: 信号组合是关键：少量样本的混合估计器就能显著提升不确定性估计性能，且数学领域由于RLVR风格的后训练而表现最佳。不确定性收益主要来自信号组合而非大规模采样。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimation is critical for deploying reasoning language models, yet remains poorly understood under extended chain-of-thought reasoning. We study parallel sampling as a fully black-box approach using verbalized confidence and self-consistency. Across three reasoning models and 17 tasks spanning mathematics, STEM, and humanities, we characterize how these signals scale.
  Both self-consistency and verbalized confidence scale in reasoning models, but self-consistency exhibits lower initial discrimination and lags behind verbalized confidence under moderate sampling. Most uncertainty gains, however, arise from signal combination: with just two samples, a hybrid estimator improves AUROC by up to $+12$ on average and already outperforms either signal alone even when scaled to much larger budgets, after which returns diminish. These effects are domain-dependent: in mathematics, the native domain of RLVR-style post-training, reasoning models achieve higher uncertainty quality and exhibit both stronger complementarity and faster scaling than in STEM or humanities.

</details>


### [199] [Implicit Patterns in LLM-Based Binary Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19138)
*Qiang Li,XiangRui Zhang,Haining Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究首次大规模分析了LLM在二进制漏洞分析中的多步推理过程，发现了四种主导的隐式模式，这些模式构成了LLM推理的抽象机制，为构建更可靠的分析系统奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的二进制漏洞分析通常采用迭代、多步推理的方式，但人们对其如何组织数百个推理步骤的探索过程了解有限，主要受限于上下文窗口和隐式的token级行为。需要系统研究LLM在多步推理中形成的结构化模式。

Method: 研究分析了521个二进制文件和99,563个推理步骤，通过追踪级别的分析识别LLM推理过程中的隐式模式。研究关注token级别的行为模式，而不是显式的控制流或预定义启发式方法。

Result: 发现了四种主导的隐式模式：早期剪枝、路径依赖锁定、针对性回溯和知识引导优先级排序。这些模式形成了稳定的结构化系统，具有不同的时间角色和可测量特征。

Conclusion: 该研究首次系统性地描述了LLM驱动的二进制分析过程，揭示了LLM推理中的结构化隐式模式，为构建更可靠的二进制分析系统提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Binary vulnerability analysis is increasingly performed by LLM-based agents in an iterative, multi-pass manner, with the model as the core decision-maker. However, how such systems organize exploration over hundreds of reasoning steps remains poorly understood, due to limited context windows and implicit token-level behaviors. We present the first large-scale, trace-level study showing that multi-pass LLM reasoning gives rise to structured, token-level implicit patterns. Analyzing 521 binaries with 99,563 reasoning steps, we identify four dominant patterns: early pruning, path-dependent lock-in, targeted backtracking, and knowledge-guided prioritization that emerge implicitly from reasoning traces. These token-level implicit patterns serve as an abstraction of LLM reasoning: instead of explicit control-flow or predefined heuristics, exploration is organized through implicit decisions regulating path selection, commitment, and revision. Our analysis shows these patterns form a stable, structured system with distinct temporal roles and measurable characteristics. Our results provide the first systematic characterization of LLM-driven binary analysis and a foundation for more reliable analysis systems.

</details>


### [200] [D5P4: Partition Determinantal Point Process for Diversity in Parallel Discrete Diffusion Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19146)
*Jonathan Lys,Vincent Gripon,Bastien Pasdeloup,Axel Marmoret,Lukas Mauch,Fabien Cardinaux,Ghouthi Boukli Hacene*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出D5P4方法，通过基于行列式点过程的广义波束搜索框架，在离散扩散模型中实现生成多样性与质量的平衡控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型在文本生成中前景广阔，但其解码方法研究不足。现有的扩散解码技术对批次内多样性控制有限，而自回归模型的波束搜索等方法无法直接应用于迭代去噪过程。

Method: 提出广义波束搜索框架，支持并行生成候选序列和模块化波束选择目标。具体实现D5P4方法，将选择步骤建模为行列式点过程的MAP推断，利用可扩展的贪心求解器，实现模型概率与目标多样性间的显式权衡。

Result: 在自由文本生成和问答任务上的实验表明，D5P4在保持竞争力的生成质量的同时，显著提升了生成多样性，优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: D5P4为离散扩散模型提供了有效的多样性控制解码框架，实现了生成质量与多样性的平衡，具有多GPU兼容性和接近零计算开销的优势。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are promising alternatives to autoregressive approaches for text generation, yet their decoding methods remain under-studied. Standard decoding methods for autoregressive models, such as beam search, do not directly apply to iterative denoising, and existing diffusion decoding techniques provide limited control over in-batch diversity. To bridge this gap, we introduce a generalized beam-search framework for discrete diffusion that generates candidates in parallel and supports modular beam-selection objectives. As a diversity-focused instantiation, we propose D5P4, which formulates the selection step as MAP inference over a Determinantal Point Process. Leveraging a scalable greedy solver, D5P4 maintains multi-GPU compatibility and enables an explicit trade-off between model probability and target diversity with near-zero compute overhead. Experiments on free-form generation and question answering demonstrate that D5P4 improves diversity over strong baselines while maintaining competitive generation quality.

</details>


### [201] [cuGenOpt: A GPU-Accelerated General-Purpose Metaheuristic Framework for Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19163)
*Yuyang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: cuGenOpt是一个GPU加速的通用元启发式框架，通过CUDA架构、用户自定义算子和Python API，在通用性、性能和易用性三个维度上同时取得突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组合优化方法在通用性、性能和易用性之间存在根本性权衡，无法同时满足这三个维度的需求。物流、调度和资源分配等领域需要能兼顾这三方面的解决方案。

Method: 采用"一个块演化一个解"的CUDA架构，包含统一编码抽象、两级自适应算子选择和硬件感知资源管理。提供用户自定义算子注册接口，以及基于JIT编译的纯Python API和LLM辅助建模工具。

Result: 在三种GPU架构上测试五个主题套件，相比通用MIP求解器性能提升数个数量级，在n=150实例上与专用求解器竞争，TSP-442在30秒内达到4.73%差距。12种问题类型在5种编码变体上达到最优解。

Conclusion: cuGenOpt成功解决了组合优化中通用性、性能和易用性的权衡问题，通过GPU加速和灵活的架构设计，为实际应用提供了高效的解决方案框架。

Abstract: Combinatorial optimization problems arise in logistics, scheduling, and resource allocation, yet existing approaches face a fundamental trade-off among generality, performance, and usability. We present cuGenOpt, a GPU-accelerated general-purpose metaheuristic framework that addresses all three dimensions simultaneously.
  At the engine level, cuGenOpt adopts a "one block evolves one solution" CUDA architecture with a unified encoding abstraction (permutation, binary, integer), a two-level adaptive operator selection mechanism, and hardware-aware resource management. At the extensibility level, a user-defined operator registration interface allows domain experts to inject problem-specific CUDA search operators. At the usability level, a JIT compilation pipeline exposes the framework as a pure-Python API, and an LLM-based modeling assistant converts natural-language problem descriptions into executable solver code.
  Experiments across five thematic suites on three GPU architectures (T4, V100, A800) show that cuGenOpt outperforms general MIP solvers by orders of magnitude, achieves competitive quality against specialized solvers on instances up to n=150, and attains 4.73% gap on TSP-442 within 30s. Twelve problem types spanning five encoding variants are solved to optimality. Framework-level optimizations cumulatively reduce pcb442 gap from 36% to 4.73% and boost VRPTW throughput by 75-81%.
  Code: https://github.com/L-yang-yang/cugenopt

</details>


### [202] [Box Maze: A Process-Control Architecture for Reliable LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19182)
*Zou Qiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Box Maze框架，通过三层认知控制架构（记忆锚定、结构化推理、边界执行）来增强大语言模型的推理过程完整性，在对抗条件下将边界失效率从约40%降至1%以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽然具有强大的生成能力，但在对抗性提示下容易产生幻觉和不可靠的推理。现有的安全方法（如RLHF和输出过滤）主要在行为层面操作，缺乏明确的架构机制来确保推理过程的完整性。

Method: 提出Box Maze框架，将LLM推理分解为三个明确层次：记忆锚定（确保推理基于事实基础）、结构化推理（强制逻辑一致性）、边界执行（防止越界推理）。通过模拟评估，在多个异构LLM系统（DeepSeek-V3、Doubao、Qwen）上进行渐进边界侵蚀场景测试。

Result: 在n=50个对抗场景中，明确的认知控制层提高了边界维护的一致性，架构约束将边界失效率从基线RLHF的约40%降低到对抗条件下低于1%。

Conclusion: 虽然当前验证是基于模拟的，但这些初步结果表明，过程级控制可能为提高大语言模型推理的可靠性提供了一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong generative capabilities but remain vulnerable to hallucination and unreliable reasoning under adversarial prompting. Existing safety approaches -- such as reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) and output filtering -- primarily operate at the behavioral level and may lack explicit architectural mechanisms for enforcing reasoning process integrity.
  This paper proposes the Box Maze framework, a conceptual process-control architecture that decomposes LLM reasoning into three explicit layers: memory grounding, structured inference, and boundary enforcement. We introduce preliminary simulation-based evaluation involving progressive boundary erosion scenarios across multiple heterogeneous LLM systems (DeepSeek-V3, Doubao, Qwen). Results from n=50 adversarial scenarios suggest that explicit cognitive control layers may improve consistency in boundary maintenance, with architectural constraints reducing boundary failure rates from approximately 40% (baseline RLHF) to below 1% under adversarial conditions.
  While current validation is simulation-based, these preliminary results indicate that process-level control may offer a promising direction for improving reliability in large language model reasoning.

</details>


### [203] [OS-Themis: A Scalable Critic Framework for Generalist GUI Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19191)
*Zehao Li,Zhenyu Wu,Yibo Zhao,Bowen Yang,Jingjing Xie,Zhaoyang Liu,Zhoumianze Liu,Kaiming Jin,Jianze Liang,Zonglin Li,Feng Wu,Bowen Zhou,Zun Wang,Zichen Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OS-Themis是一个可扩展且准确的多智能体批评框架，通过将轨迹分解为可验证的里程碑来改进GUI智能体的强化学习奖励函数，在AndroidWorld实验中实现了10.3%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在GUI智能体中有潜力，但训练对奖励函数质量高度敏感。现有的奖励方法难以同时实现可扩展性和性能，需要更好的奖励机制来改进GUI智能体在随机环境中的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出OS-Themis多智能体批评框架：1）将轨迹分解为可验证的里程碑以隔离关键决策证据；2）采用审查机制严格审核证据链后再做出最终判断；3）引入OmniGUIRewardBench（OGRBench）作为跨平台GUI结果奖励基准。

Result: 1）所有评估模型在OS-Themis下都达到最佳性能；2）在AndroidWorld实验中，OS-Themis支持在线RL训练时带来10.3%的改进；3）在自训练循环中用于轨迹验证和过滤时带来6.9%的增益。

Conclusion: OS-Themis通过分解轨迹和严格审查证据链，显著改进了GUI智能体的强化学习性能，展示了驱动智能体进化的潜力，为GUI环境中的奖励设计提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has the potential to improve the robustness of GUI agents in stochastic environments, yet training is highly sensitive to the quality of the reward function. Existing reward approaches struggle to achieve both scalability and performance. To address this, we propose OS-Themis, a scalable and accurate multi-agent critic framework. Unlike a single judge, OS-Themis decomposes trajectories into verifiable milestones to isolate critical evidence for decision making and employs a review mechanism to strictly audit the evidence chain before making the final verdict. To facilitate evaluation, we further introduce OmniGUIRewardBench (OGRBench), a holistic cross-platform benchmark for GUI outcome rewards, where all evaluated models achieve their best performance under OS-Themis. Extensive experiments on AndroidWorld show that OS-Themis yields a 10.3% improvement when used to support online RL training, and a 6.9% gain when used for trajectory validation and filtering in the self-training loop, highlighting its potential to drive agent evolution.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [204] [Uncovering Latent Phase Structures and Branching Logic in Locomotion Policies: A Case Study on HalfCheetah](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18084)
*Daisuke Yasui,Toshitaka Matsuki,Hiroshi Sato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究验证了在运动控制任务中，深度强化学习训练的策略会自主形成可解释的相位结构，并通过EBM方法分析了各相位的决策机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习在运动控制任务中表现出色，但其决策过程是黑盒，难以理解。周期性运动（如行走）存在隐式运动相位（如支撑相和摆动相），本研究假设训练好的运动控制策略也可能形成人类可解释的相位结构。

Method: 在MuJoCo的HalfCheetah-v5环境中，通过状态相似性和后续转移一致性，将训练策略获得的状态转移序列聚合成语义相位。然后使用可解释提升机（EBMs）近似每个相位对应的状态和动作，分析相位依赖的决策机制。

Result: 训练策略生成的状态序列展现出周期性相位转移结构以及相位分支。通过EBM分析揭示了策略在各相位中关注的状态特征和动作输出控制方式。

Conclusion: 神经网络策略虽然常被视为黑盒，但能自主获取可解释的相位结构和逻辑分支机制，为理解深度强化学习决策过程提供了新视角。

Abstract: In locomotion control tasks, Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) has demonstrated high performance; however, the decision-making process of the learned policy remains a black box, making it difficult for humans to understand. On the other hand, in periodic motions such as walking, it is well known that implicit motion phases exist, such as the stance phase and the swing phase. Focusing on this point, this study hypothesizes that a policy trained for locomotion control may also represent a phase structure that is interpretable by humans. To examine this hypothesis in a controlled setting, we consider a locomotion task that is amenable to observing whether a policy autonomously acquires temporally structured phases through interaction with the environment. To verify this hypothesis, in the MuJoCo locomotion benchmark HalfCheetah-v5, the state transition sequences acquired by a policy trained for walking control through interaction with the environment were aggregated into semantic phases based on state similarity and consistency of subsequent transitions. As a result, we demonstrated that the state sequences generated by the trained policy exhibit periodic phase transition structures as well as phase branching. Furthermore, by approximating the states and actions corresponding to each semantic phase using Explainable Boosting Machines (EBMs), we analyzed phase-dependent decision making-namely, which state features the policy function attends to and how it controls action outputs in each phase. These results suggest that neural network-based policies, which are often regarded as black boxes, can autonomously acquire interpretable phase structures and logical branching mechanisms.

</details>


### [205] [Final Report for the Workshop on Robotics & AI in Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18130)
*Juan P Wachs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CARE研讨会汇集专家讨论医疗机器人AI发展，强调建立国家卓越中心，解决数据、评估、监管等障碍，推动智能系统在临床转化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗机器人AI领域发展迅速，但面临数据不足、评估标准缺失、监管路径不明确、人才培训不足等障碍，需要国家层面的协调研究来加速临床转化。

Method: 通过研讨会形式，汇集研究人员、临床医生、产业创新者和政府利益相关者，通过主题演讲、小组讨论和分组会议，识别关键问题并形成共识。

Result: 达成建立国家医疗AI与机器人卓越中心（CARE）的共识，确定优先研究方向：人机协作、可信自主性、数字孪生、多模态感知、生成式AI临床集成等。

Conclusion: 需要建立国家级协调机制，解决数据、测试平台、监管和人才等系统性障碍，以充分发挥医疗机器人AI在手术、诊断、康复等领域的变革潜力。

Abstract: The CARE Workshop on Robotics and AI in Medicine, held on December 1, 2025 in Indianapolis, convened leading researchers, clinicians, industry innovators, and federal stakeholders to shape a national vision for advancing robotics and artificial intelligence in healthcare. The event highlighted the accelerating need for coordinated research efforts that bridge engineering innovation with real clinical priorities, emphasizing safety, reliability, and translational readiness with an emphasis on the use of robotics and AI to achieve this readiness goal.
  Across keynotes, panels, and breakout sessions, participants underscored critical gaps in data availability, standardized evaluation methods, regulatory pathways, and workforce training that hinder the deployment of intelligent robotic systems in surgical, diagnostic, rehabilitative, and assistive contexts. Discussions emphasized the transformative potential of AI enabled robotics to improve precision, reduce provider burden, expand access to specialized care, and enhance patient outcomes particularly in undeserved regions and high risk procedural domains. Special attention was given to austere settings, disaster and relief and military settings.
  The workshop demonstrated broad consensus on the urgency of establishing a national Center for AI and Robotic Excellence in medicine (CARE). Stakeholders identified priority research thrusts including human robot collaboration, trustworthy autonomy, simulation and digital twins, multi modal sensing, and ethical integration of generative AI into clinical workflows. Participants also articulated the need for high quality datasets, shared test beds, autonomous surgical systems, clinically grounded benchmarks, and sustained interdisciplinary training mechanisms.

</details>


### [206] [GoalVLM: VLM-driven Object Goal Navigation for Multi-Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18210)
*MoniJesu James,Amir Atef Habel,Aleksey Fedoseev,Dzmitry Tsetserokou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GoalVLM是一个用于零样本、开放词汇物体导航的多智能体框架，无需任务特定训练，通过视觉语言模型进行语义推理，在HM3D场景中达到55.8%的子任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物体导航方法主要针对地面机器人且局限于封闭词汇集，多智能体方法依赖预计算概率图，无法泛化到测试时的新目标。需要能够处理自由形式语言目标的零样本导航系统。

Method: 集成视觉语言模型到决策循环，使用SAM3进行文本提示检测和分割，SpaceOM进行空间推理。每个智能体通过深度投影体素拼接构建BEV语义地图，Goal Projector将检测结果通过校准深度反投影到地图中。约束引导推理层通过结构化提示链评估前沿区域。

Result: 在GOAT-Bench val_unseen数据集上（360个多子任务场景，1032个顺序物体目标子任务），2个智能体的GoalVLM达到55.8%子任务成功率和18.3%SPL，与最先进方法竞争且无需任务特定训练。消融研究证实了VLM引导前沿推理和深度投影目标定位的贡献。

Conclusion: GoalVLM展示了将视觉语言模型集成到多智能体导航框架中的可行性，实现了零样本开放词汇物体导航，通过语义先验和空间推理实现了竞争性性能，为无需任务特定训练的可泛化导航系统提供了新方向。

Abstract: Object-goal navigation has traditionally been limited to ground robots with closed-set object vocabularies. Existing multi-agent approaches depend on precomputed probabilistic graphs tied to fixed category sets, precluding generalization to novel goals at test time.
  We present GoalVLM, a cooperative multi-agent framework for zero-shot, open-vocabulary object navigation. GoalVLM integrates a Vision-Language Model (VLM) directly into the decision loop, SAM3 for text-prompted detection and segmentation, and SpaceOM for spatial reasoning, enabling agents to interpret free-form language goals and score frontiers via zero-shot semantic priors without retraining. Each agent builds a BEV semantic map from depth-projected voxel splatting, while a Goal Projector back-projects detections through calibrated depth into the map for reliable goal localization. A constraint-guided reasoning layer evaluates frontiers through a structured prompt chain (scene captioning, room-type classification, perception gating, multi-frontier ranking), injecting commonsense priors into exploration.
  We evaluate GoalVLM on GOAT-Bench val_unseen (360 multi-subtask episodes, 1032 sequential object-goal subtasks, HM3D scenes), where each episode requires navigating to a chain of 5-7 open-vocabulary targets. GoalVLM with N=2 agents achieves 55.8% subtask SR and 18.3% SPL, competitive with state-of-the-art methods while requiring no task-specific training. Ablation studies confirm the contributions of VLM-guided frontier reasoning and depth-projected goal localization.

</details>


### [207] [ReDAG-RT: Global Rate-Priority Scheduling for Real-Time Multi-DAG Execution in ROS 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18238)
*Md. Mehedi Hasan,Rafid Mostafiz,Bikash Kumar Paul,Md. Abir Hossain,Ziaur Rahman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReDAGRT：一个用户空间的全局调度框架，用于在未修改的ROS 2中实现确定性多DAG执行，通过速率优先级调度减少截止时间错失率和响应时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 默认ROS 2执行器使用尽力而为的调度方式，缺乏跨DAG优先级执行，导致回调争用、结构性优先级反转和截止时间不稳定，限制了在时间关键和安全关键系统中的部署。

Method: 提出ReDAGRT框架，引入速率优先级驱动的全局就绪队列，按激活速率排序回调，强制执行每DAG并发边界，缓解跨图优先级反转，无需修改ROS 2 API、执行器接口或底层操作系统调度器。

Result: 在ROS 2 Humble环境中测试显示：截止时间错失率降低29.7%，第99百分位响应时间减少42.9%，在可比利用率下比MultiThreadedExecutor提升13.7%，非对称每DAG并发边界进一步减少40.8%的干扰。

Conclusion: ReDAGRT证明确定性可分析的多DAG调度可以完全在ROS 2用户空间执行层实现，为安全关键系统中的实时机器人中间件提供了实用基础。

Abstract: ROS 2 has become a dominant middleware for robotic systems, where perception, estimation, planning, and control pipelines are structured as directed acyclic graphs of callbacks executed under a shared executor. However, default ROS 2 executors use best-effort dispatch without cross-DAG priority enforcement, leading to callback contention, structural priority inversion, and deadline instability under concurrent workloads. These limitations restrict deployment in time-critical and safety-sensitive cyber-physical systems. This paper presents ReDAGRT, a user-space global scheduling framework for deterministic multi-DAG execution in unmodified ROS 2. The framework introduces a Rate-Priority driven global ready queue that orders callbacks by activation rate, enforces per-DAG concurrency bounds, and mitigates cross-graph priority inversion without modifying the ROS 2 API, executor interface, or underlying operating system scheduler. We formalize a multi-DAG task model for ROS 2 callback pipelines and analyze cross-DAG interference under Rate-Priority scheduling. Response-time recurrences and schedulability conditions are derived within classical Rate-Monotonic theory. Experiments in a ROS 2 Humble environment compare ReDAGRT against SingleThreadedExecutor and MultiThreadedExecutor using synthetic multi-DAG workloads. Results show up to 29.7 percent reduction in deadline miss rate, 42.9 percent reduction in 99th percentile response time, and 13.7 percent improvement over MultiThreadedExecutor under comparable utilization. Asymmetric per-DAG concurrency bounds further reduce interference by 40.8 percent. These results demonstrate that deterministic and analyzable multi-DAG scheduling can be achieved entirely in the ROS 2 user-space execution layer, providing a practical foundation for real-time robotic middleware in safety-critical systems.

</details>


### [208] [Rapid Adaptation of Particle Dynamics for Generalized Deformable Object Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18246)
*Bohan Wu,Roberto Martín-Martín,Li Fei-Fei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAPiD方法将快速运动适应(RMA)扩展到可变形物体操作，通过两阶段学习：1)基于物体动力学嵌入的视觉运动策略学习，2)从非特权信息推断嵌入，实现真实世界转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形物体操作面临未知动力学的挑战，传统RMA方法主要处理刚体参数（质量、位置），但可变形物体的动力学还包括形状变化。需要开发能够适应未知可变形物体动力学的方法。

Method: RAPiD采用两阶段方法：第一阶段在仿真中学习基于物体动力学嵌入（包括质量和真实粒子位置）的视觉运动策略；第二阶段学习从非特权信息（机器人视觉观测和动作）推断该嵌入，实现向真实世界的转移。

Result: 在22自由度的移动机械臂上，RAPiD在两个基于视觉的可变形物体移动操作任务中实现了80%以上的成功率，适用于不同物体动力学、类别和实例。

Conclusion: 通过利用可变形物体在仿真中的真实粒子位置来编码形状变化，成功将RMA扩展到可变形物体操作领域，实现了从仿真到真实世界的有效转移。

Abstract: We address the challenge of learning to manipulate deformable objects with unknown dynamics. In non-rigid objects, the dynamics parameters define how they react to interactions -- how they stretch, bend, compress, and move -- and they are critical to determining the optimal actions to perform a manipulation task successfully. In other robotic domains, such as legged locomotion and in-hand rigid object manipulation, state-of-the-art approaches can handle unknown dynamics using Rapid Motor Adaptation (RMA). Through a supervised procedure in simulation that encodes each rigid object's dynamics, such as mass and position, these approaches learn a policy that conditions actions on a vector of latent dynamic parameters inferred from sequences of state-actions. However, in deformable object manipulation, the object's dynamics not only includes its mass and position, but also how the shape of the object changes. Our key insight is that the recent ground-truth particle positions of a deformable object in simulation capture changes in the object's shape, making it possible to extend RMA to deformable object manipulation. This key insight allows us to develop RAPiD, a two-phase method that learns to perform real-robot deformable object mobile manipulation by: 1) learning a visuomotor policy conditioned on the object's dynamics embedding, which is encoded from the object's privileged information in simulation, such as its mass and ground-truth particle positions, and 2) learning to infer this embedding using non-privileged information instead, such as robot visual observations and actions, so that the learned policy can transfer to the real world. On a mobile manipulator with 22 degrees of freedom, RAPiD enables over 80%+ success rates across two vision-based deformable object mobile manipulation tasks in the real world, under various object dynamics, categories, and instances.

</details>


### [209] [Manufacturing Micro-Patterned Surfaces with Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18260)
*Annalisa T. Taylor,Malachi Landis,Ping Guo,Todd D. Murphey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 多机器人系统使用遍历控制算法协调制造微图案表面，降低金属表面摩擦系数，实现规模化生产


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前制造技术无法规模化生产微图案表面，因为高成本设备和低效的扫描覆盖技术限制了应用

Method: 使用多个配备图案化工具的机器人，通过遍历控制算法协调覆盖任务，机器人通过压缩轨迹历史表示进行通信和任务分配

Result: 机器人能够通过通信协调完成复杂覆盖目标，实验证明机器人制造的图案能降低金属表面摩擦系数

Conclusion: 分布式多机器人系统能够协调制造以前无法规模化生产的产品，为微图案表面制造提供了新途径

Abstract: Applying micro-patterns to surfaces has been shown to impart useful physical properties such as drag reduction and hydrophobicity. However, current manufacturing techniques cannot produce micro-patterned surfaces at scale due to high-cost machinery and inefficient coverage techniques such as raster-scanning. In this work, we use multiple robots, each equipped with a patterning tool, to manufacture these surfaces. To allow these robots to coordinate during the patterning task, we use the ergodic control algorithm, which specifies coverage objectives using distributions. We demonstrate that robots can divide complicated coverage objectives by communicating compressed representations of their trajectory history both in simulations and experimental trials. Further, we show that robot-produced patterning can lower the coefficient of friction of metallic surfaces. This work demonstrates that distributed multi-robot systems can coordinate to manufacture products that were previously unrealizable at scale.

</details>


### [210] [SG-CoT: An Ambiguity-Aware Robotic Planning Framework using Scene Graph Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18271)
*Akshat Rana,Peeyush Agarwal,K. P. S. Rana,Amarjit Malhotra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SG-CoT是一个两阶段框架，通过迭代查询场景图来检测和澄清LLM机器人规划中的歧义，显著提升规划可靠性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型作为机器人规划器时，歧义性是一个主要挑战。现有方法在处理环境中的模糊指令时容易出错，需要一种能主动检测和澄清歧义的机制来提高规划可靠性。

Method: 提出Scene Graph-Chain-of-Thought (SG-CoT)两阶段框架：1) 从输入观察构建结构化场景图，捕捉对象、属性和关系；2) LLM配备检索函数查询与指令相关的场景图部分，将推理过程锚定在观察中。框架还允许LLM识别歧义源并向用户或其他机器人提出澄清问题。

Result: 实验表明SG-CoT始终优于先前方法：问题准确率至少提升10%，单智能体环境成功率至少提升4%，多智能体环境成功率至少提升15%。验证了其在更通用机器人规划中的有效性。

Conclusion: SG-CoT通过将LLM推理过程锚定在结构化场景图表示中，有效解决了机器人规划中的歧义问题，显著提高了规划可靠性和泛化能力，为LLM在机器人领域的应用提供了更稳健的框架。

Abstract: Ambiguity poses a major challenge to large language models (LLMs) used as robotic planners. In this letter, we present Scene Graph-Chain-of-Thought (SG-CoT), a two-stage framework where LLMs iteratively query a scene graph representation of the environment to detect and clarify ambiguities. First, a structured scene graph representation of the environment is constructed from input observations, capturing objects, their attributes, and relationships with other objects. Second, the LLM is equipped with retrieval functions to query portions of the scene graph that are relevant to the provided instruction. This grounds the reasoning process of the LLM in the observation, increasing the reliability of robotic planners under ambiguous situations. SG-CoT also allows the LLM to identify the source of ambiguity and pose a relevant disambiguation question to the user or another robot. Extensive experimentation demonstrates that SG-CoT consistently outperforms prior methods, with a minimum of 10% improvement in question accuracy and a minimum success rate increase of 4% in single-agent and 15% in multi-agent environments, validating its effectiveness for more generalizable robot planning.

</details>


### [211] [Offload or Overload: A Platform Measurement Study of Mobile Robotic Manipulation Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18284)
*Sara Pohland,Xenofon Foukas,Ganesh Ananthanarayanan,Andrey Kolobov,Sanjeev Mehrotra,Bozidar Radunovic,Ankit Verma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文首次对移动机器人操作工作负载在板载、边缘和云GPU平台上的性能进行了测量研究，发现完整工作负载在小型板载GPU上不可行，大型板载GPU会显著缩短电池续航，而卸载计算则面临网络延迟和带宽挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型虽然提升了移动机器人操作的性能，但带来了巨大的计算开销。目前缺乏对这些工作负载在不同计算平台（板载、边缘、云）上实际性能的系统测量研究，这对于设计高效的机器人推理系统至关重要。

Method: 首次对移动机器人操作工作负载进行全面的测量研究，评估其在三种GPU平台上的性能：板载GPU（小型和大型）、边缘计算和云平台。分析计算卸载的可行性和挑战，并量化机器人车队共享计算的机会与风险。

Result: 1) 完整工作负载无法在小型板载GPU上运行；2) 大型板载GPU会使机器人电池续航缩短数小时；3) 计算卸载虽能缓解约束，但网络延迟会降低任务精度，带宽需求使简单的云卸载不切实际；4) 机器人车队共享计算存在机会但也面临挑战。

Conclusion: 该测量研究为设计移动机器人的推理系统提供了关键见解，揭示了板载计算的局限性、计算卸载的权衡以及车队共享计算的潜力，对物理AI系统的实际部署具有重要意义。

Abstract: Mobile robotic manipulation--the ability of robots to navigate spaces and interact with objects--is a core capability of physical AI. Foundation models have led to breakthroughs in their performance, but at a significant computational cost. We present the first measurement study of mobile robotic manipulation workloads across onboard, edge, and cloud GPU platforms. We find that the full workload stack is infeasible to run on smaller onboard GPUs, while larger onboard GPUs drain robot batteries several hours faster. Offloading alleviates these constraints but introduces its own challenges, as additional network latency degrades task accuracy, and the bandwidth requirement makes naive cloud offloading impractical. Finally, we quantify opportunities and pitfalls of sharing compute across robot fleets. We believe our measurement study will be crucial to designing inference systems for mobile robots.

</details>


### [212] [Sparse3DTrack: Monocular 3D Object Tracking Using Sparse Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18298)
*Nikhil Gosala,B. Ravi Kiran,Senthil Yogamani,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 首个稀疏监督的单目3D目标跟踪框架，通过将任务分解为2D查询匹配和3D几何估计两个子问题，利用时空一致性从稀疏标注生成高质量3D伪标签，显著提升跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的单目3D目标跟踪方法完全依赖密集的3D标注，这些标注获取成本高且难以扩展。为了解决这一根本限制，提出了首个稀疏监督框架。

Method: 将任务分解为两个顺序子问题：2D查询匹配和3D几何估计。两个组件都利用图像序列的时空一致性来增强稀疏标注样本，学习丰富的2D和3D场景表示。基于这些学习到的线索，模型自动生成整个视频的高质量3D伪标签，将稀疏监督转化为密集的3D跟踪标注。

Result: 在KITTI和nuScenes数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法显著提高了跟踪性能，在每条轨迹最多使用4个真实标注的情况下，性能提升高达15.50个百分点。

Conclusion: 提出的稀疏监督框架有效解决了单目3D目标跟踪中标注成本高的问题，通过将稀疏监督转化为密集伪标签，使现有完全监督跟踪器能够在极端标签稀疏条件下有效运行。

Abstract: Monocular 3D object tracking aims to estimate temporally consistent 3D object poses across video frames, enabling autonomous agents to reason about scene dynamics. However, existing state-of-the-art approaches are fully supervised and rely on dense 3D annotations over long video sequences, which are expensive to obtain and difficult to scale. In this work, we address this fundamental limitation by proposing the first sparsely supervised framework for monocular 3D object tracking. Our approach decomposes the task into two sequential sub-problems: 2D query matching and 3D geometry estimation. Both components leverage the spatio-temporal consistency of image sequences to augment a sparse set of labeled samples and learn rich 2D and 3D representations of the scene. Leveraging these learned cues, our model automatically generates high-quality 3D pseudolabels across entire videos, effectively transforming sparse supervision into dense 3D track annotations. This enables existing fully-supervised trackers to effectively operate under extreme label sparsity. Extensive experiments on the KITTI and nuScenes datasets demonstrate that our method significantly improves tracking performance, achieving an improvement of up to 15.50 p.p. while using at most four ground truth annotations per track.

</details>


### [213] [Proprioceptive-only State Estimation for Legged Robots with Set-Coverage Measurements of Learned Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18308)
*Abhijeet M. Kulkarni,Ioannis Poulakakis,Guoquan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于集合覆盖噪声建模的纯本体感知状态估计框架，用于足式机器人，相比高斯噪声假设方法在真实噪声场景下保持一致性且不易漂移


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯本体感知状态估计对足式机器人具有吸引力，因为它计算成本低且不受感知退化条件影响。现有方法使用学习测量模型并与IMU数据融合，假设高斯噪声，但这种假设在有限训练数据下容易失效，导致估计不一致和潜在发散。

Method: 提出一个纯本体感知状态估计框架，使用集合覆盖语句来表征测量噪声，不假设任何分布。开发了一种实用且计算成本低的方法，将这些集合覆盖测量与高斯滤波器系统性地结合使用。

Result: 在仿真和两个真实世界四足机器人数据集上验证了该方法。与高斯基线方法比较显示，所提方法在真实噪声场景下保持一致性且不易漂移。

Conclusion: 提出的基于集合覆盖噪声建模的纯本体感知状态估计框架，相比传统高斯噪声假设方法，在真实噪声场景下表现更稳健，能够保持估计一致性并减少漂移问题。

Abstract: Proprioceptive-only state estimation is attractive for legged robots since it is computationally cheaper and is unaffected by perceptually degraded conditions. The history of joint-level measurements contains rich information that can be used to infer the dynamics of the system and subsequently produce navigational measurements. Recent approaches produce these estimates with learned measurement models and fuse with IMU data, under a Gaussian noise assumption. However, this assumption can easily break down with limited training data and render the estimates inconsistent and potentially divergent. In this work, we propose a proprioceptive-only state estimation framework for legged robots that characterizes the measurement noise using set-coverage statements that do not assume any distribution. We develop a practical and computationally inexpensive method to use these set-coverage measurements with a Gaussian filter in a systematic way. We validate the approach in both simulation and two real-world quadrupedal datasets. Comparison with the Gaussian baselines shows that our proposed method remains consistent and is not prone to drift under real noise scenarios.

</details>


### [214] [DriveVLM-RL: Neuroscience-Inspired Reinforcement Learning with Vision-Language Models for Safe and Deployable Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18315)
*Zilin Huang,Zihao Sheng,Zhengyang Wan,Yansong Qu,Junwei You,Sicong Jiang,Sikai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DriveVLM-RL：一种神经科学启发的双通路框架，将视觉语言模型集成到强化学习中，用于安全可部署的自动驾驶，在训练时使用VLM进行语义奖励学习，部署时移除VLM以保证实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习方法依赖人工设计的奖励或稀疏碰撞信号，无法捕捉安全驾驶所需的丰富上下文理解，且不安全探索不可避免。视觉语言模型具有语义理解能力，但推理延迟高且易产生幻觉，难以直接用于实时车辆控制。

Method: 提出双通路架构：静态通路使用CLIP进行连续空间安全评估，动态通路使用轻量检测器和大型VLM进行注意力门控多帧语义风险推理。通过分层奖励合成机制融合语义信号与车辆状态，异步训练管道将昂贵VLM推理与环境交互解耦。所有VLM组件仅在离线训练时使用，部署时移除以保证实时性。

Result: 在CARLA模拟器实验中，碰撞避免、任务成功率和不同交通场景下的泛化能力显著提升，在没有显式碰撞惩罚的设置下也表现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DriveVLM-RL为将基础模型集成到自动驾驶中提供了实用范式，不损害实时可行性，展示了神经科学启发的双通路架构在安全决策中的有效性。

Abstract: Ensuring safe decision-making in autonomous vehicles remains a fundamental challenge despite rapid advances in end-to-end learning approaches. Traditional reinforcement learning (RL) methods rely on manually engineered rewards or sparse collision signals, which fail to capture the rich contextual understanding required for safe driving and make unsafe exploration unavoidable in real-world settings. Recent vision-language models (VLMs) offer promising semantic understanding capabilities; however, their high inference latency and susceptibility to hallucination hinder direct application to real-time vehicle control. To address these limitations, this paper proposes DriveVLM-RL, a neuroscience-inspired framework that integrates VLMs into RL through a dual-pathway architecture for safe and deployable autonomous driving. The framework decomposes semantic reward learning into a Static Pathway for continuous spatial safety assessment using CLIP-based contrasting language goals, and a Dynamic Pathway for attention-gated multi-frame semantic risk reasoning using a lightweight detector and a large VLM. A hierarchical reward synthesis mechanism fuses semantic signals with vehicle states, while an asynchronous training pipeline decouples expensive VLM inference from environment interaction. All VLM components are used only during offline training and are removed at deployment, ensuring real-time feasibility. Experiments in the CARLA simulator show significant improvements in collision avoidance, task success, and generalization across diverse traffic scenarios, including strong robustness under settings without explicit collision penalties. These results demonstrate that DriveVLM-RL provides a practical paradigm for integrating foundation models into autonomous driving without compromising real-time feasibility. Demo video and code are available at: https://zilin-huang.github.io/DriveVLM-RL-website/

</details>


### [215] [ManiDreams: An Open-Source Library for Robust Object Manipulation via Uncertainty-aware Task-specific Intuitive Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18336)
*Gaotian Wang,Kejia Ren,Andrew S. Morgan,Kaiyu Hang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ManiDreams是一个用于不确定性感知操作规划的模块化框架，通过可组合的抽象实现分布状态表示、后端无关的动态预测和声明式约束规范，在不确定环境中提升机器人操作的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界机器人操作本质上存在不确定性，传统动态模型主要关注最小化预测误差，而非在规划循环中表示、传播和约束不确定性。需要一种能够集成处理感知、参数和结构不确定性的框架。

Method: 提出ManiDreams框架，包含分布状态表示、后端无关动态预测和声明式约束规范的可组合抽象。采用样本-预测-约束循环包装任何基础策略，评估候选动作的分布结果，无需重新训练即可增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在ManiSkill任务上的实验表明，ManiDreams在各种扰动下保持鲁棒性能，而强化学习基线性能显著下降。在推动、抓取、捕捉和真实世界部署的示例展示了跨不同策略、优化器、物理后端和执行器的灵活性。

Conclusion: ManiDreams为不确定性感知操作规划提供了一个实用的模块化框架，通过集成不确定性表示和约束机制，在不确定环境中实现了更鲁棒的机器人操作性能。

Abstract: Dynamics models, whether simulators or learned world models, have long been central to robotic manipulation, but most focus on minimizing prediction error rather than confronting a more fundamental challenge: real-world manipulation is inherently uncertain. We argue that robust manipulation under uncertainty is fundamentally an integration problem: uncertainties must be represented, propagated, and constrained within the planning loop, not merely suppressed during training. We present and open-source ManiDreams, a modular framework for uncertainty-aware manipulation planning over intuitive physics models. It realizes this integration through composable abstractions for distributional state representation, backend-agnostic dynamics prediction, and declarative constraint specification for action optimization. The framework explicitly addresses three sources of uncertainty: perceptual, parametric, and structural. It wraps any base policy with a sample-predict-constrain loop that evaluates candidate actions against distributional outcomes, adding robustness without retraining. Experiments on ManiSkill tasks show that ManiDreams maintains robust performance under various perturbations where the RL baseline degrades significantly. Runnable examples on pushing, picking, catching, and real-world deployment demonstrate flexibility across different policies, optimizers, physics backends, and executors. The framework is publicly available at https://github.com/Rice-RobotPI-Lab/ManiDreams

</details>


### [216] [Shifting Uncertainty to Critical Moments: Towards Reliable Uncertainty Quantification for VLA Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18342)
*Yanchuan Tang,Taowen Wang,Yuefei Chen,Boxuan Zhang,Qiang Guan,Ruixiang Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于VLA机器人策略的改进不确定性量化方法，通过滑动窗口最大池化、运动感知稳定性加权和自由度自适应校准，显著提升故障预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在机器人控制中缺乏可靠的内省能力。传统方法使用token级不确定性的平均值，但均值聚合会稀释安全关键的不确定性尖峰。成功轨迹可能包含局部高熵段，而失败轨迹可能在大部分时间低熵，仅在故障发生时出现短暂尖峰。

Method: 1) 使用基于最大值的滑动窗口池化来保留瞬态风险信号；2) 应用运动感知稳定性加权，强调与不稳定行为相关的高频动作振荡；3) 通过贝叶斯优化进行自由度自适应校准，优先考虑运动学关键轴。

Result: 在LIBERO基准测试中，该方法显著提高了故障预测准确性，并产生更可靠的故障检测信号，可支持下游人机交互干预。

Conclusion: 提出的不确定性量化方法能有效捕捉VLA机器人策略中的瞬态风险信号，为安全关键应用提供更可靠的故障预测能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable general-purpose robotic policies by mapping visual observations and language instructions to low-level actions, but they often lack reliable introspection. A common practice is to compute a token-level uncertainty signal and take its mean over a rollout. However, mean aggregation can dilute short-lived but safety-critical uncertainty spikes in continuous control. In particular, successful rollouts may contain localized high-entropy segments due to benign noise or non-critical micro-adjustments, while failure rollouts can appear low-entropy for most timesteps and only exhibit brief spikes near the onset of failure. We propose a unified uncertainty quantification approach for predicting rollout success versus failure that (1) uses max-based sliding window pooling to preserve transient risk signals, (2) applies motion-aware stability weighting to emphasize high-frequency action oscillations associated with unstable behaviors, and (3) performs DoF-adaptive calibration via Bayesian Optimization to prioritize kinematically critical axes. Experiments on the LIBERO benchmark show that our method substantially improves failure prediction accuracy and yields more reliable signals for failure detection, which can support downstream human-in-the-loop interventions.

</details>


### [217] [HRI-SA: A Multimodal Dataset for Online Assessment of Human Situational Awareness during Remote Human-Robot Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18344)
*Hashini Senaratne,Richard Attfield,Samith Widhanapathirana,David Howard,Cecile Paris,Dana Kulic,Leimin Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了HRI-SA数据集，用于评估人机协作中的情境感知，通过眼动追踪等特征有效检测感知延迟


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高工作负荷和动态条件下，操作员常出现情境感知(SA)缺口，但现有SA测量方法要么干扰任务流程，要么无法捕捉实时波动，且缺乏公开数据集支持在线SA评估

Method: 创建HRI-SA多模态数据集，包含30名参与者在搜索救援人机协作场景中的眼动、瞳孔直径、生物信号、用户交互和机器人数据，通过测量援助需求出现到解决的时间获取两种SA延迟(感知和理解)的真实标签

Result: 眼动特征能有效分类感知SA延迟(召回率88.91%，F1分数67.63%)，结合上下文数据融合后性能提升(召回率91.51%，F1分数80.38%)

Conclusion: 贡献了首个支持人机协作任务中SA系统评估的公开数据集，展示了通用眼动特征在远程人机协作中连续感知SA延迟检测的潜力

Abstract: Maintaining situational awareness (SA) is critical in human-robot teams. Yet, under high workload and dynamic conditions, operators often experience SA gaps. Automated detection of SA gaps could provide timely assistance for operators. However, conventional SA measures either disrupt task flow or cannot capture real-time fluctuations, limiting their operational utility. To the best of our knowledge, no publicly available dataset currently supports the systematic evaluation of online human SA assessment in human-robot teaming. To advance the development of online SA assessment tools, we introduce HRI-SA, a multimodal dataset from 30 participants in a realistic search-and-rescue human-robot teaming context, incorporating eye movements, pupil diameter, biosignals, user interactions, and robot data. The experimental protocol included predefined events requiring timely operator assistance, with ground truth SA latency of two types (perceptual and comprehension) systematically obtained by measuring the time between assistance need onset and resolution. We illustrate the utility of this dataset by evaluating standard machine learning models for detecting perceptual SA latencies using generic eye-tracking features and contextual features. Results show that eye-tracking features alone effectively classified perceptual SA latency (recall=88.91%, F1=67.63%) using leave-one-group-out cross-validation, with performance improved through contextual data fusion (recall=91.51%, F1=80.38%). This paper contributes the first public dataset supporting the systematic evaluation of SA throughout a human-robot teaming mission, while also demonstrating the potential of generic eye-tracking features for continuous perceptual SA latency detection in remote human-robot teaming.

</details>


### [218] [Multi-material Direct Ink Writing and Embroidery for Stretchable Wearable Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18354)
*Lukas Cha,Ryman Hashem,Ria Prakash,Tanguy Declety,Wenze Zhang,Liang He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种结合多材料直接墨水书写与自动刺绣的纺织兼容制造工艺，用于在服装中直接嵌入可拉伸应变传感器


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴传感系统在运动表现追踪、康复和损伤预防方面的需求日益增长，需要结合舒适性、耐用性和精确运动检测的智能服装

Method: 采用多材料直接墨水书写与自动刺绣相结合的制造工艺，顺序打印硅胶-碳脂-硅胶堆叠结构，通过刺绣同时实现机械固定和电气接口

Result: 传感器拉伸性达120%，60%应变内近似线性响应(R²=0.99)，应变系数31.4，滞后22.9%；80次循环测试显示良好稳定性；成功集成到肘部和膝部护套进行关节角度监测

Conclusion: 通过刺绣集成同时解决机械固定和电气接口问题，为将印刷可拉伸电子器件整合到纺织品系统中提供了可重复且可扩展的途径

Abstract: The development of wearable sensing systems for sports performance tracking, rehabilitation, and injury prevention has driven growing demand for smart garments that combine comfort, durability, and accurate motion detection. This paper presents a textile-compatible fabrication workflow that integrates multi-material direct ink writing with automated embroidery to create stretchable strain sensors directly embedded into garments. The process combines sequential multi-material printing of a silicone-carbon grease-silicone stack with automated embroidery that provides both mechanical fixation and electrical interfacing in a single step. The resulting hybrid sensor demonstrates stretchability up to 120% strain while maintaining electrical continuity, with approximately linear behaviour up to 60% strain (R^2 = 0.99), a gauge factor of 31.4, and hysteresis of 22.9%. Repeated loading-unloading tests over 80 cycles show baseline and peak drift of 0.135% and 0.236% per cycle, respectively, indicating moderate cycle-to-cycle stability. Mechanical testing further confirms that the silicone-fabric interface remains intact under large deformation, with failure occurring in the textile rather than at the stitched boundary. As a preliminary proof of concept, the sensor was integrated into wearable elbow and knee sleeves for joint angle monitoring, showing a clear correlation between normalised resistance change and bending angle. By addressing both mechanical fixation and electrical interfacing through embroidery-based integration, this approach provides a reproducible and scalable pathway for incorporating printed stretchable electronics into textile systems for motion capture and soft robotic applications.

</details>


### [219] [Contact Status Recognition and Slip Detection with a Bio-inspired Tactile Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18370)
*Chengxiao He,Wenhui Yang,Hongliang Zhao,Jiacheng Lv,Yuzhe Shao,Longhui Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于多模态触觉反馈的机器人滑移检测方法，使用仿生五指手和分布式触觉传感器，通过接触状态识别而非阈值方法实现高精度滑移检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作中，特别是对于易碎和光滑物体，需要精确控制抓握力。过大力量会损坏物体，过小力量会导致滑移和掉落。在非结构化环境中，滑移检测和及时预防是必要的。

Method: 使用仿生五指手的多模态触觉反馈，将24个触觉通道分布在软皮肤中。将滑移检测问题转化为接触状态识别，结合分箱技术。通过离散小波变换处理信号，提取17个特征，使用最优120个特征进行状态识别。

Result: 在三种不同滑动速度和六种材料下，测试准确率达到96.39%。应用于四种未见新材料时，仍达到91.95%的高准确率，验证了方法的泛化能力。基于训练好的接触状态识别模型，滑移检测性能得到验证。

Conclusion: 该方法通过接触状态识别而非传统阈值方法，实现了高精度和强泛化能力的滑移检测，为机器人精细操作提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: Stable and reliable grasp is critical to robotic manipulations especially for fragile and glazed objects, where the grasp force requires precise control as too large force possibly damages the objects while small force leads to slip and fall-off. Although it is assumed the objects to manipulate is grasped firmly in advance, slip detection and timely prevention are necessary for a robot in unstructured and universal environments. In this work, we addressed this issue by utilizing multimodal tactile feedback from a five-fingered bio-inspired hand. Motivated by human hands, the tactile sensing elements were distributed and embedded into the soft skin of robotic hand, forming 24 tactile channels in total. Different from the threshold method that was widely employed in most existing works, we converted the slip detection problem to contact status recognition in combination with binning technique first and then detected the slip onset time according to the recognition results. After the 24-channel tactile signals passed through discrete wavelet transform, 17 features were extracted from different time and frequency bands. With the optimal 120 features employed for status recognition, the test accuracy reached 96.39% across three different sliding speeds and six kinds of materials. When applied to four new unseen materials, a high accuracy of 91.95% was still achieved, which further validated the generalization of our proposed method. Finally, the performance of slip detection is verified based on the trained model of contact status recognition.

</details>


### [220] [Graph-of-Constraints Model Predictive Control for Reactive Multi-agent Task and Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18400)
*Anastasios Manganaris,Jeremy Lu,Ahmed H. Qureshi,Suresh Jagannathan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GoC-MPC：一种用于多智能体任务与运动规划的新框架，通过约束图集成模型预测控制，支持部分有序任务、动态智能体协调和扰动恢复，仅需视觉观测即可在线适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有处理几何约束序列的方法难以应对部分有序任务和动态智能体分配，通常假设静态分配且无法适应扰动导致的任务分配变化，限制了多智能体TAMP在实际场景中的应用。

Method: 提出约束图模型预测控制（GoC-MPC），将约束序列框架与MPC集成，通过跟踪3D关键点定义约束，支持部分有序任务和动态智能体协调，仅依赖视觉观测在线适应，无需训练数据或环境模型。

Result: 实验表明GoC-MPC相比现有基线方法，在多种多智能体操作任务中实现了更高的成功率、显著更快的TAMP计算速度和更短的整体路径，证明其在实际扰动下的高效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GoC-MPC为多智能体操作任务提供了一种高效、鲁棒的解决方案，能够处理部分有序任务、动态智能体协调和实时扰动恢复，仅需视觉观测即可在线适应，无需先验模型或训练数据。

Abstract: Sequences of interdependent geometric constraints are central to many multi-agent Task and Motion Planning (TAMP) problems. However, existing methods for handling such constraint sequences struggle with partially ordered tasks and dynamic agent assignments. They typically assume static assignments and cannot adapt when disturbances alter task allocations. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Graph-of-Constraints Model Predictive Control (GoC-MPC), a generalized sequence-of-constraints framework integrated with MPC. GoC-MPC naturally supports partially ordered tasks, dynamic agent coordination, and disturbance recovery. By defining constraints over tracked 3D keypoints, our method robustly solves diverse multi-agent manipulation tasks-coordinating agents and adapting online from visual observations alone, without relying on training data or environment models. Experiments demonstrate that GoC-MPC achieves higher success rates, significantly faster TAMP computation, and shorter overall paths compared to recent baselines, establishing it as an efficient and robust solution for multi-agent manipulation under real-world disturbances. Our supplementary video and code can be found at https://sites.google.com/view/goc-mpc/home .

</details>


### [221] [Efficient and Versatile Quadrupedal Skating: Optimal Co-design via Reinforcement Learning and Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18408)
*Hanwen Wang,Zhenlong Fang,Josiah Hanna,Xiaobin Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 四足机器人被动轮滑冰的硬件-控制协同设计方法，通过双层优化框架（贝叶斯优化搜索机械设计，强化学习训练控制策略）实现高效多功能的滑冰运动


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动轮滑冰能减少腿部惯性和提高能量效率，特别是在高速情况下，但缺乏直接轮驱动使得机械设计与控制紧密耦合，限制了该模态的潜力

Method: 提出双层优化框架：上层使用贝叶斯优化搜索机械设计空间，下层使用强化学习为每个候选设计训练电机控制策略

Result: 设计-策略对不仅优于人工设计的基线，还表现出多功能行为，如曲棍球式急停（侧转最大化摩擦实现快速制动）和自对齐运动（自动重新定向以提高行进方向的能量效率）

Conclusion: 该方法首次实现了四足机器人动态滑冰运动的系统级研究，展示了硬件-控制协同设计在解锁被动轮滑冰全部潜力方面的有效性

Abstract: In this paper, we present a hardware-control co-design approach that enables efficient and versatile roller skating on quadrupedal robots equipped with passive wheels. Passive-wheel skating reduces leg inertia and improves energy efficiency, particularly at high speeds. However, the absence of direct wheel actuation tightly couples mechanical design and control. To unlock the full potential of this modality, we formulate a bilevel optimization framework: an upper-level Bayesian Optimization searches the mechanical design space, while a lower-level Reinforcement Learning trains a motor control policy for each candidate design. The resulting design-policy pairs not only outperform human-engineered baselines, but also exhibit versatile behaviors such as hockey stop (rapid braking by turning sideways to maximize friction) and self-aligning motion (automatic reorientation to improve energy efficiency in the direction of travel), offering the first system-level study of dynamic skating motion on quadrupedal robots.

</details>


### [222] [MemoAct: Atkinson-Shiffrin-Inspired Memory-Augmented Visuomotor Policy for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18494)
*Liufan Tan,Jiale Li,Gangshan Jing*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MemoAct：受Atkinson-Shiffrin记忆模型启发的分层记忆策略，通过无损短期记忆实现精确任务状态跟踪，压缩长期记忆实现鲁棒长时程保留，在MemoryRTBench基准测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于记忆的机器人策略通常依赖简单的观察窗口扩展，难以同时实现精确的任务状态跟踪和鲁棒的长时程记忆保留，这限制了在记忆依赖任务中的性能。

Method: 提出MemoAct分层记忆策略，受Atkinson-Shiffrin记忆模型启发，采用不同记忆层级：无损短期记忆用于精确任务状态跟踪，压缩长期记忆用于长时程保留。基于RoboTwin 2.0构建MemoryRTBench评估基准。

Result: 在模拟和真实场景的大量实验中，MemoAct在任务状态跟踪和长时程记忆保留方面均优于现有的马尔可夫基线和历史感知策略，表现出卓越性能。

Conclusion: MemoAct通过分层记忆架构有效解决了机器人策略中同时实现精确状态跟踪和长时程记忆保留的挑战，为记忆增强型机器人策略提供了新思路。

Abstract: Memory-augmented robotic policies are essential in handling memory-dependent tasks. However, existing approaches typically rely on simple observation window extensions, struggling to simultaneously achieve precise task state tracking and robust long-horizon retention. To overcome these challenges, inspired by the Atkinson-Shiffrin memory model, we propose MemoAct, a hierarchical memory-based policy that leverages distinct memory tiers to tackle specific bottlenecks. Specifically, lossless short-term memory ensures precise task state tracking, while compressed long-term memory enables robust long-horizon retention. To enrich the evaluation landscape, we construct MemoryRTBench based on RoboTwin 2.0, specifically tailored to assess policy capabilities in task state tracking and long-horizon retention. Extensive experiments across simulated and real-world scenarios demonstrate that MemoAct achieves superior performance compared to both existing Markovian baselines and history-aware policies. The project page is \href{https://tlf-tlf.github.io/MemoActPage/}{available}.

</details>


### [223] [Robotic Agentic Platform for Intelligent Electric Vehicle Disassembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18520)
*Zachary Allen,Max Conway,Lyle Antieau,Allen Ponraj,Nikolaus Correll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAPID平台用于EV电池包自动化拆解，集成机械臂、视觉感知和AI代理编程，在真实回收场景中测试了多种螺栓拆卸策略和LLM控制方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动汽车电池回收需求迫切，但当前拆解主要依赖人工且设计变异性高，需要开发可扩展的自动化解决方案。

Method: 开发RAPID平台，包含龙门式工业机械臂、RGB-D视觉系统和自动螺母拆卸工具。采用开放词汇目标检测识别组件，测试三种螺栓拆卸策略，并引入基于LLM的AI代理规范进行机器人编程。

Result: 开放词汇检测达到0.9757 mAP50；螺栓拆卸成功率：示教位姿97%、单次视觉执行57%、视觉伺服83%；基于工具的LLM接口实现100%任务完成率，而自动ROS服务发现失败率达43.3%。

Conclusion: RAPID平台为系统研究人机协作、代理机器人编程和自主拆解工作流程提供了开源基础，是迈向可扩展机器人电池回收的重要实践基础。

Abstract: Electric vehicles (EV) create an urgent need for scalable battery recycling, yet disassembly of EV battery packs remains largely manual due to high design variability. We present our Robotic Agentic Platform for Intelligent Disassembly (RAPID), designed to investigate perception-driven manipulation, flexible automation, and AI-assisted robot programming in realistic recycling scenarios. The system integrates a gantry-mounted industrial manipulator, RGB-D perception, and an automated nut-running tool for fastener removal on a full-scale EV battery pack. An open-vocabulary object detection pipeline achieves 0.9757 mAP50, enabling reliable identification of screws, nuts, busbars, and other components. We experimentally evaluate (n=204) three one-shot fastener removal strategies: taught-in poses (97% success rate, 24 min duration), one-shot vision execution (57%, 29 min), and visual servoing (83%, 36 min), comparing success rate and disassembly time for the battery's top cover fasteners. To support flexible interaction, we introduce agentic AI specifications for robotic disassembly tasks, allowing LLM agents to translate high-level instructions into robot actions through structured tool interfaces and ROS services. We evaluate SmolAgents with GPT-4o-mini and Qwen 3.5 9B/4B on edge hardware. Tool-based interfaces achieve 100% task completion, while automatic ROS service discovery shows 43.3% failure rates, highlighting the need for structured robot APIs for reliable LLM-driven control. This open-source platform enables systematic investigation of human-robot collaboration, agentic robot programming, and increasingly autonomous disassembly workflows, providing a practical foundation for research toward scalable robotic battery recycling.

</details>


### [224] [Scaling Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning for Robot VLAs with Generative 3D Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18532)
*Andrew Choi,Xinjie Wang,Zhizhong Su,Wei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 利用3D世界生成模型和语言驱动场景设计，通过生成数百个多样化的交互场景，对视觉语言动作模型进行强化学习微调，在保持泛化能力的同时显著提升性能，并成功实现仿真到现实的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言动作模型的强化学习微调面临两难：在真实世界训练会限制模型的泛化能力（场景和物体多样性有限），而在仿真中训练又需要高昂的场景设计成本。需要一种既能保持模型泛化能力又能降低人工成本的方法。

Method: 结合3D世界生成模型和语言驱动场景设计，自动生成数百个包含独特物体和背景的多样化交互场景。从预训练的模仿学习基线开始，在生成的场景中进行可扩展、高度并行的策略学习，并使用领域随机化实现仿真到现实的迁移。

Result: 仿真成功率从9.7%提升到79.8%，任务完成时间加速1.25倍。仿真到现实迁移成功率达到75%（从21.7%提升），现实世界任务完成时间加速1.13倍。消融研究表明增加场景多样性直接改善零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 利用3D世界生成模型可以为视觉语言动作模型的强化学习微调提供大规模、多样化的训练场景，在保持模型泛化能力的同时显著提升性能，并成功实现仿真到现实的迁移，为解决真实世界训练的限制提供了有效途径。

Abstract: The strong performance of large vision-language models (VLMs) trained with reinforcement learning (RL) has motivated similar approaches for fine-tuning vision-language-action (VLA) models in robotics. Many recent works fine-tune VLAs directly in the real world to avoid addressing the sim-to-real gap. While real-world RL circumvents sim-to-real issues, it inherently limits the generality of the resulting VLA, as scaling scene and object diversity in the physical world is prohibitively difficult. This leads to the paradoxical outcome of transforming a broadly pretrained model into an overfitted, scene-specific policy. Training in simulation can instead provide access to diverse scenes, but designing those scenes is also costly. In this work, we show that VLAs can be RL fine-tuned without sacrificing generality and with reduced labor by leveraging 3D world generative models. Using these models together with a language-driven scene designer, we generate hundreds of diverse interactive scenes containing unique objects and backgrounds, enabling scalable and highly parallel policy learning. Starting from a pretrained imitation baseline, our approach increases simulation success from 9.7% to 79.8% while achieving a 1.25$\times$ speedup in task completion time. We further demonstrate successful sim-to-real transfer enabled by the quality of the generated digital twins together with domain randomization, improving real-world success from 21.7% to 75% and achieving a 1.13$\times$ speedup. Finally, we further highlight the benefits of leveraging the effectively unlimited data from 3D world generative models through an ablation study showing that increasing scene diversity directly improves zero-shot generalization.

</details>


### [225] [Inductance-Based Force Self-Sensing in Fiber-Reinforced Pneumatic Twisted-and-Coiled Actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18555)
*Yunsong Zhang,Tianlin Li,Mingyang Yang,Feitian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种集成导电镍线的自感知纤维增强气动扭绞卷绕致动器，利用电感-力关系实现力估计和位移推断，开发非线性混合观测器解决电感-力映射模糊问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统FR-PTCAs存在强滞后性和缺乏内在本体感知能力，限制了有效的闭环控制，需要开发具有内在力感知能力的自感知致动器

Method: 集成导电镍线构建自感知FR-PTCA，利用电感-力关系建立参数化自感知模型，开发结合扩展卡尔曼滤波和约束优化的非线性混合观测器

Result: 实验表明电感-力关系具有确定性且滞后性低，提出的方法实现了与外部负载相当的力估计精度，在不同负载条件下保持鲁棒性能

Conclusion: 通过集成导电镍线和开发非线性混合观测器，成功实现了FR-PTCA的内在力感知和状态估计，为软体机器人闭环控制提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Fiber-reinforced pneumatic twisted-and-coiled actuators (FR-PTCAs) offer high power density and compliance but their strong hysteresis and lack of intrinsic proprioception limit effective closed-loop control. This paper presents a self-sensing FR-PTCA integrated with a conductive nickel wire that enables intrinsic force estimation and indirect displacement inference via inductance feedback. Experimental characterization reveals that the inductance of the actuator exhibits a deterministic, low-hysteresis inductance-force relationship at constant pressures, in contrast to the strongly hysteretic inductance-length behavior. Leveraging this property, this paper develops a parametric self-sensing model and a nonlinear hybrid observer that integrates an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) with constrained optimization to resolve the ambiguity in the inductance-force mapping and estimate actuator states.
